    def body(self, ops):

        ge = ops.ge(index_expr, constant)

    def masked_subblock1(self, ops):

class op33_loop_body:
    var_ranges = {p0: 241, p1: 15, p2: 64}
    index0 = p2
    index1 = 960*p0 + 64*p1 + p2
    index2 = p0
    index3 = p2 - 32
    index4 = 960*p0 + 64*p1 + p2 - 32
    index5 = 960*p0 + 64*p1 + p2 + 32
    def body(self, ops):
        get_index = self.get_index('index0')
        index_expr = ops.index_expr(get_index, torch.int64)
        constant = ops.constant(32, torch.int64)
        ge = ops.ge(index_expr, constant)
        masked_subblock1 = self.masked_subblock1(ge, 0.0)
        get_index_1 = self.get_index('index0')
        index_expr_1 = ops.index_expr(get_index_1, torch.int64)
        constant_1 = ops.constant(32, torch.int64)
        lt = ops.lt(index_expr_1, constant_1)
        masked_subblock2 = self.masked_subblock2(lt, 0.0)
        constant_2 = ops.constant(nan, torch.float32)
        where = ops.where(lt, masked_subblock2, constant_2)
        where_1 = ops.where(ge, masked_subblock1, where)
        get_index_2 = self.get_index('index1')
        store = ops.store('buf33', get_index_2, where_1, None)
        return store
    def masked_subblock1(self, ops):
        get_index = self.get_index('index1')
        load = ops.load('buf30', get_index)
        to_dtype = ops.to_dtype(load, torch.float32, src_dtype = torch.bfloat16)
        get_index_1 = self.get_index('index2')
        load_1 = ops.load('buf6', get_index_1)
        constant = ops.constant(1, torch.int64)
        sub = ops.sub(load_1, constant)
        to_dtype_1 = ops.to_dtype(sub, torch.float32, src_dtype = torch.int64)
        get_index_2 = self.get_index('index3')
        index_expr = ops.index_expr(get_index_2, torch.float32)
        constant_1 = ops.constant(0.03125, torch.float32)
        mul = ops.mul(index_expr, constant_1)
        constant_2 = ops.constant(10000.0, torch.float32)
        pow_1 = ops.pow(constant_2, mul)
        truediv = ops.truediv(to_dtype_1, pow_1)
        cos = ops.cos(truediv)
        mul_1 = ops.mul(to_dtype, cos)
        get_index_3 = self.get_index('index4')
        load_2 = ops.load('buf30', get_index_3)

class op325_loop_body:
    var_ranges = {p0: 241, p1: 15, p2: 64}
    index0 = p2
    index1 = 960*p0 + 64*p1 + p2
    index2 = p0
    index3 = p2 - 32
    index4 = 960*p0 + 64*p1 + p2 - 32
    index5 = 960*p0 + 64*p1 + p2 + 32
    def body(self, ops):
        get_index = self.get_index('index0')
        index_expr = ops.index_expr(get_index, torch.int64)
        constant = ops.constant(32, torch.int64)
        ge = ops.ge(index_expr, constant)
        masked_subblock1 = self.masked_subblock1(ge, 0.0)
        get_index_1 = self.get_index('index0')
        index_expr_1 = ops.index_expr(get_index_1, torch.int64)
        constant_1 = ops.constant(32, torch.int64)
        lt = ops.lt(index_expr_1, constant_1)
        masked_subblock2 = self.masked_subblock2(lt, 0.0)
        constant_2 = ops.constant(nan, torch.float32)
        where = ops.where(lt, masked_subblock2, constant_2)
        where_1 = ops.where(ge, masked_subblock1, where)
        get_index_2 = self.get_index('index1')
        store = ops.store('buf325', get_index_2, where_1, None)

        index_expr = ops.index_expr(get_index, torch.int64)
